```sql
            AND submitted_at IS NOT NULL));

ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_appeal_type
    CHECK (appeal_type IS NULL
        OR appeal_type IN ('DATA_ERROR', 'COEF_DISPUTE', 'FORMULA_DISPUTE', 'OTHER'));

ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_appeal_status
    CHECK (appeal_status IS NULL
        OR appeal_status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'PROCESSING', 'RESOLVED',
                             'REJECTED', 'WITHDRAWN'));

ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_appeal_resolved
    CHECK (appeal_status IS NULL OR appeal_status <> 'RESOLVED'
        OR (verify_result IS NOT NULL AND verify_note IS NOT NULL
            AND char_length(btrim(verify_note)) >= 20
            AND resolution_type IS NOT NULL AND reply_to_staff IS NOT NULL
            AND resolved_at IS NOT NULL AND resolved_by IS NOT NULL));

ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_appeal_resolution
    CHECK (resolution_type IS NULL OR resolution_type IN (
        'NO_CHANGE', 'ADJUST_UP', 'ADJUST_DOWN', 'RECALC', 'DATA_CORRECT'));

-- 调整相关字段只在 ADJUST/REVERSAL 行出现
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_fields
    CHECK ((record_type = 'APPEAL')
        OR (adjust_amount IS NOT NULL
            AND adjust_reason IS NOT NULL
            AND char_length(btrim(adjust_reason)) >= 10
            AND original_amount_snap IS NOT NULL
            AND final_amount_snap IS NOT NULL
            AND applied_by IS NOT NULL
            AND applied_by_name IS NOT NULL));

ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_method
    CHECK (adjust_method IS NULL
        OR adjust_method IN ('DIRECT_ADJUST', 'RECALC'));

-- ★核心：调整后最终应付快照 = 原绩效快照 + 调整额（证明未改动原值）
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_closed
    CHECK (record_type = 'APPEAL'
        OR final_amount_snap = GREATEST(original_amount_snap + adjust_amount, 0.00));

-- 调减不得使最终应付为负
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_nonneg
    CHECK (record_type = 'APPEAL'
        OR adjust_amount >= -(original_amount_snap));

-- 大额调整与调减必须有审批单号（规范：> 500.00 或调减）
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_approval
    CHECK (record_type = 'APPEAL'
        OR (abs(adjust_amount) <= 500.00 AND adjust_amount >= 0)
        OR approval_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 撤回必须有被撤回单号
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_reversal
    CHECK ((record_type <> 'REVERSAL') OR reversal_of IS NOT NULL);

-- 附件数组合法
ALTER TABLE m17_perf_adjust
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m17_perf_adjust_attachments
    CHECK (jsonb_typeof(attachment_urls) = 'array');

COMMENT ON TABLE m17_perf_adjust IS '调整与申诉：record_type 区分 APPEAL 申诉 / ADJUST 调整 / REVERSAL 撤回；只增不改，永不覆盖 m17_perf_result.original_amount';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.record_type IS 'APPEAL 申诉记录 / ADJUST 调整记录 / REVERSAL 撤回记录（撤回通过追加反向调整实现）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.original_amount_snap IS '调整时点的原绩效快照，用于证明原值未被改动，并作为最终应付的计算基准';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.final_amount_snap IS '调整后最终应付快照，CHECK 强制 = GREATEST(原绩效快照 + 调整额, 0.00)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.adjust_amount IS '调整额，正为调增、负为调减；调减绝对值不得超过原绩效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.approval_no IS 'M20 审批单号，调整额绝对值 > 500.00 或为调减时必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.reversal_of IS '被撤回的调整单号，仅 record_type=REVERSAL 时填写';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m17_perf_adjust.applied_at IS '记录时刻，永不修改（只增表）';
```

**DDL**

```sql
CREATE TABLE m17_perf_adjust (
    id                  BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    record_type         VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
    record_no           VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    period              VARCHAR(7)    NOT NULL,
    result_id           BIGINT        NOT NULL,
    calc_batch_no       VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    staff_id            BIGINT        NOT NULL,
    staff_no            VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    staff_name          VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    dept_name           VARCHAR(64),
    appeal_type         VARCHAR(24),
    appeal_content      VARCHAR(2000),
    appeal_status       VARCHAR(16),
    attachment_urls     JSONB         NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    submitted_at        TIMESTAMP,
    verify_result       BOOLEAN,
    verify_note         VARCHAR(1000),
    resolution_type     VARCHAR(24),
    reply_to_staff      VARCHAR(1000),
    resolved_at         TIMESTAMP,
    resolved_by         BIGINT,
    resolved_by_name    VARCHAR(64),
    original_amount_snap NUMERIC(18,2),
    adjust_amount       NUMERIC(18,2),
    final_amount_snap   NUMERIC(18,2),
    adjust_reason       VARCHAR(1000),
    adjust_method       VARCHAR(24),
    appeal_no           VARCHAR(32),
    approval_no         VARCHAR(64),
    reversal_of         VARCHAR(32),
    applied_by          BIGINT,
    applied_by_name     VARCHAR(64),
    applied_at          TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    trace_id            VARCHAR(32)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m17_perf_adjust_no
    ON m17_perf_adjust (record_type, record_no);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m17_perf_appeal_open
    ON m17_perf_adjust (staff_id, period)
    WHERE record_type = 'APPEAL'
      AND appeal_status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'PROCESSING');
CREATE INDEX idx_m17_perf_adjust_result
    ON m17_perf_adjust (result_id, record_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_m17_perf_adjust_period
    ON m17_perf_adjust (period, record_type, applied_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_m17_perf_adjust_staff
    ON m17_perf_adjust (staff_id, period DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_m17_perf_appeal_pending
    ON m17_perf_adjust (period, appeal_status)
    WHERE appeal_status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'PROCESSING');
CREATE INDEX idx_m17_perf_adjust_approval
    ON m17_perf_adjust (approval_no) WHERE approval_no IS NOT NULL;
```

**保护调整记录只增的权限：**

```sql
-- 调整记录只增不改：回收 UPDATE / DELETE
REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON m17_perf_adjust FROM m17_app;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON m17_perf_adjust TO m17_app;
```

**设计说明：**

1. **为什么申诉与调整合到一张表？** 两者是「一件事的两面」：申诉提出诉求，调整是处置结果，共同构成一次修正的完整记录。合表后「查这条调整源于哪次申诉」是一次同表查询，且 `appeal_no` 字段天然建立对应关系。若分表，每次追溯都要跨表 JOIN，且两张表的结构 80% 重复（人都要员工信息、期间、关联结果）。

2. **为什么 `original_amount_snap` 要落库？** 它是「我没有改动原值」的**举证材料**。调整行里同时记录「当时原绩效是多少」与「调整后是多少」，即使有人事后去改 `m17_perf_result`，本条记录仍能证明调整时的基准。这与会计的「原始凭证」理念一致。

3. **为什么 `REVERSAL` 要独立成一种类型而不是 UPDATE 原调整行？** 撤回一次调整（如申诉被上级推翻）在业务上是「新发生的事件」，而非「原来那件事没发生过」。追加反向行既保留了「曾经调增过」的事实，又实现了「净效果归零」，审计上完整。这也与凭证的「红冲」逻辑一致。

4. **为什么 `uk_m17_perf_appeal_open` 只约束未结案状态？** 同一员工同一期间只允许一条进行中的申诉（防止重复提交刷屏），但已结案的申诉不限制——若第一次申诉被驳回后员工补充材料再提，属于合理诉求，应允许。

5. **为什么调减阈值与调增同为 500.00？** 调减的风险实际上更高（直接影响职工到手金额，易引发劳动争议），规范要求「> 500.00 **或调减**」都必须审批，即调减**无论金额大小都要审批**。CHECK 用 `(abs(adjust_amount) <= 500 AND adjust_amount >= 0) OR approval_no IS NOT NULL` 精确表达这一点。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 |
|---|---|
| 申诉数 | 约 12 条/月（约 2.6% 职工会申诉） |
| 调整数 | 约 8 条/月（约 70% 申诉属实） |
| 年增长 | 约 240 行 |
| 10 年累计 | 约 2,400 行 |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 1.6 KB |
| 表大小 | 约 3.8 MB |
| 分区 | 不分区 |

### 4.12 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

```python
# app/models/m17/performance.py
"""M17-07 绩效核算 —— ORM 模型"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, Index, Integer, Numeric,
    SmallInteger, String, TIMESTAMP, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.models.base import Base


class M17PerfScheme(Base):
    """m17_perf_scheme（绩效方案主档）

    版本化配置：同方案号仅一个 ACTIVE 版本（部分唯一索引保证）。
    ACTIVE 方案不可原地修改，必须生成新版本。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_scheme"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    scheme_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    previous_version: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    scheme_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")

    # 适用范围
    apply_scope: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DEPT")
    apply_depts: Mapped[List[int]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)
    apply_staff_types: Mapped[List[str]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                         default=list)
    apply_titles: Mapped[List[str]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)

    # 生效期与分配
    effective_from: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    effective_to: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    pool_ratio: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                default=Decimal("0.150000"))
    retained_ratio_cap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.200000"))
    allow_negative_incentive: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                           default=False)
    show_rank: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    # 科室系数映射
    dept_coefficient_mapping: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)
    dept_coef_min: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0.900000"))
    dept_coef_max: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("1.100000"))

    # 质量系数映射
    quality_coefficient_mapping: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)
    quality_component_weights: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=dict)
    quality_floor_coefficient: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6))

    # 变更治理与审计
    change_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    activated_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    deactivated_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    version_lock: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_by: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    created_by_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_scheme_no_ver", "scheme_no", "version", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_scheme_active", "scheme_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'ACTIVE' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_scheme_status", "status", text("effective_from DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_scheme_effective", "effective_from", "effective_to",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("scheme_no ~ '^PS[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{3}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT','ACTIVE','INACTIVE','EXPIRED')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_status"),
        CheckConstraint("apply_scope IN ('ALL','DEPT','STAFF_TYPE','TITLE')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_scope"),
        CheckConstraint("effective_to >= effective_from",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_period"),
        CheckConstraint("pool_ratio > 0 AND pool_ratio <= 0.500000",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_pool_ratio"),
        CheckConstraint("dept_coef_min > 0 AND dept_coef_max >= dept_coef_min",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_scheme_dept_bounds"),
    )


class M17PerfIndicator(Base):
    """m17_perf_indicator（方案指标与权重）

    启用指标的权重合计必须 = 1.000000，由 PerfCalcService 在计算前强制校验。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_indicator"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    scheme_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    scheme_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    scheme_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    indicator_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    indicator_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    indicator_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    data_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    source_field: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    unit_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0.0000"))
    weight: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                            default=Decimal("0.000000"))
    calc_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                             default="LINEAR")
    calc_params: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    sort_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    is_enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500))
    version_lock: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_ind_scheme_code", "scheme_id", "indicator_code",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_ind_scheme_sort", "scheme_id", "sort_no",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_ind_scheme", "scheme_id", "is_enabled", "sort_no",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_ind_code", "indicator_code",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("indicator_type IN ('WORKLOAD','QUALITY','ECONOMIC',"
                        "'SERVICE','MANUAL')", name="ck_m17_perf_ind_type"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_method IN ('LINEAR','TIERED','CAPPED',"
                        "'DEDUCTION','THRESHOLD')", name="ck_m17_perf_ind_method"),
        CheckConstraint("unit_value >= 0 AND weight >= 0 AND weight <= 1",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_ind_value"),
        CheckConstraint("(calc_method = 'LINEAR' AND calc_params IS NULL) OR "
                        "(calc_method <> 'LINEAR' AND calc_params IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_ind_params"),
        CheckConstraint("indicator_code ~ '^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{1,31}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_ind_code"),
    )


class M17PerfCalcBatch(Base):
    """m17_perf_calc_batch（计算批次）

    锁定 scheme_version，保存参数快照与统计；所有绩效结果挂在该批次下。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_calc_batch"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    settle_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    period_start: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)
    period_end: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=False)

    scheme_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    scheme_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    scheme_snapshot: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                            default=dict)

    balance_basis: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                               default="CURRENT_MONTH")
    quality_basis: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                              default="CURRENT_MONTH")
    retained_handling: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                                   default="KEEP_DEPT")
    precheck_result: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)

    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")
    is_recalc: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    superseded_batch_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    headcount: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    total_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("0.00"))
    retained_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                      default=Decimal("0.00"))
    avg_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                default=Decimal("0.00"))
    zero_amount_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    manual_workload_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False,
                                                       default=0)

    publish_start_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    publish_end_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date)
    publish_working_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    published_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    paid_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)

    started_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    finished_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    duration_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer)
    error_message: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(2000))
    triggered_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    triggered_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500))

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_batch_no", "calc_batch_no", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_batch_settle", "settle_no", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_batch_running", "period", "scheme_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'DRAFT' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_batch_period", "period", "status",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_batch_status", "status", text("created_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_batch_scheme", "scheme_no", "scheme_version"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_batch_no ~ '^PB[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{4}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("settle_no ~ '^PC[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{4}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_settle_format"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT','CALCULATED','AUDITED','PUBLISHED',"
                        "'PAID','CANCELLED')", name="ck_m17_perf_batch_status"),
        CheckConstraint("headcount >= 0 AND total_amount >= 0 "
                        "AND retained_amount >= 0 AND avg_amount >= 0",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_stats"),
        CheckConstraint("status NOT IN ('PUBLISHED','PAID') OR "
                        "(publish_start_date IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND publish_end_date IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND publish_working_days >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_published"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'PAID' OR paid_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_paid"),
        CheckConstraint("is_recalc = FALSE OR superseded_batch_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_batch_recalc"),
    )


class M17PerfWorkload(Base):
    """m17_perf_workload（工作量采集明细）

    绩效计算的原料；唯一键 (calc_batch_no, staff_id, indicator_code)
    是 P-05 幂等的落地点。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_workload"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)

    staff_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    staff_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    staff_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    dept_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    dept_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)

    indicator_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    indicator_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    actual_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0.00"))
    detail: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False, default=dict)

    data_source: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    collection_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                                    default="MISSING")
    collected_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    push_batch_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    is_manual: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    manual_basis: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    manual_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    supplemented_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    supplemented_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    supplemented_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_wl_batch_staff", "calc_batch_no", "staff_id",
              "indicator_code", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_wl_batch", "calc_batch_no", "dept_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_wl_period", "period", "dept_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_wl_missing", "calc_batch_no", "collection_status",
              postgresql_where=text("collection_status IN ('MISSING','PARTIAL') "
                                    "AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("actual_value >= 0", name="ck_m17_perf_wl_value"),
        CheckConstraint("collection_status IN ('COMPLETE','PARTIAL','MISSING')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_wl_status"),
        CheckConstraint("collection_status <> 'MISSING' OR "
                        "(actual_value = 0 AND data_source IS NULL)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_wl_missing"),
        CheckConstraint("is_manual = FALSE OR (manual_basis IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(manual_basis)) >= 10 "
                        "AND manual_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND data_source = 'MANUAL')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_wl_manual"),
    )


class M17PerfResult(Base):
    """m17_perf_result（绩效结果主档）

    original_amount 有 CHECK 精确等于公式，是规范 §6.5 的数据库级保证；
    PUBLISHED 后由触发器 trg_m17_perf_result_freeze 拦截 UPDATE。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_result"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)

    staff_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    staff_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    staff_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    dept_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    dept_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)

    base_subtotal: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0.00"))
    dept_coefficient: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                       default=Decimal("1.000000"))
    quality_coefficient: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False, default=Decimal("1.000000"))
    original_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                      default=Decimal("0.00"))
    adjust_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                    default=Decimal("0.00"))
    # final_amount 为数据库生成列，ORM 侧只读
    final_amount: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2),
        server_default=text("GREATEST(original_amount + adjust_amount, 0.00)"),
        nullable=False,
    )
    dept_share_ratio: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                       default=Decimal("0.000000"))

    result_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                               default="CALCULATED")
    is_audited: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    has_manual_workload: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                       default=False)
    has_quality_default: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                       default=False)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    calculated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_result_batch_staff", "calc_batch_no", "staff_id",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_result_period_dept", "period", "dept_id",
              text("original_amount DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_result_staff", "staff_id", text("period DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_result_batch", "calc_batch_no", "is_audited",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("base_subtotal >= 0",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_subtotal"),
        CheckConstraint("dept_coefficient >= 0.900000 "
                        "AND dept_coefficient <= 1.100000",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_dept_coef"),
        CheckConstraint("quality_coefficient >= 0.850000 "
                        "AND quality_coefficient <= 1.000000",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_quality_coef"),
        CheckConstraint("original_amount >= 0",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_original"),
        # 规范 §6.5 公式的数据库级保证
        CheckConstraint("original_amount = ROUND(base_subtotal * dept_coefficient "
                        "* quality_coefficient, 2)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_amount"),
        CheckConstraint("dept_share_ratio >= 0 AND dept_share_ratio <= 1",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_share"),
        CheckConstraint("result_status IN ('DRAFT','CALCULATED','AUDITED',"
                        "'PUBLISHED','PAID','CANCELLED')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_result_status"),
    )


class M17PerfResultItem(Base):
    """m17_perf_result_item（指标得分明细）

    每个结果的逐指标得分，是可复算的最小单元。
    LINEAR 方式的得分由 CHECK 保证等于公式值。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_result_item"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    result_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    indicator_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    indicator_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)

    actual_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0.00"))
    unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    unit_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                 default=Decimal("0.0000"))
    weight: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                            default=Decimal("0.000000"))
    calc_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                             default="LINEAR")

    score: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                            default=Decimal("0.00"))
    formula_text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=False)
    data_source: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    collected_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_item_result_code", "result_id", "indicator_code",
              unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_item_result", "result_id"),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_item_batch", "calc_batch_no"),
        CheckConstraint("actual_value >= 0 AND unit_value >= 0 "
                        "AND weight >= 0 AND weight <= 1",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_item_input"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_method IN ('LINEAR','TIERED','CAPPED',"
                        "'DEDUCTION','THRESHOLD')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_item_method"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_method <> 'LINEAR' OR "
                        "score = ROUND(actual_value * unit_value * weight, 2)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_item_score_linear"),
        CheckConstraint("calc_method <> 'DEDUCTION' OR "
                        "score = ROUND(0 - actual_value * unit_value * weight, 2)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_item_score_deduction"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(formula_text)) >= 5",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_item_formula"),
    )


class M17PerfCoefficient(Base):
    """m17_perf_coefficient（系数快照）

    记录科室系数与质量系数的完整来源与映射过程，
    是回答「系数为什么是这个值」的唯一证据。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_coefficient"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    dept_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    dept_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    dept_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    coef_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)

    dept_revenue_amount: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    dept_direct_cost_amount: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    dept_allocated_cost_amount: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2))
    dept_profit_amount: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    profit_rate: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6))
    dept_allocatable_pool: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))

    quality_components: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=dict)

    source_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(14, 4), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0.0000"))
    coefficient_value: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(9, 6), nullable=False,
                                                        default=Decimal("1.000000"))
    mapped_rule: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=False)
    mapping_snapshot: Mapped[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, default=list)
    is_default: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)
    default_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_coef_batch_dept", "calc_batch_no", "dept_id",
              "coef_type", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_coef_batch", "calc_batch_no", "coef_type"),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_coef_dept", "dept_id", "period"),
        CheckConstraint("coef_type IN ('DEPT','QUALITY')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_coef_type"),
        CheckConstraint("coef_type <> 'DEPT' OR (coefficient_value >= 0.900000 "
                        "AND coefficient_value <= 1.100000)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_coef_dept_range"),
        CheckConstraint("coef_type <> 'QUALITY' OR (coefficient_value >= 0.850000 "
                        "AND coefficient_value <= 1.000000)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_coef_quality_range"),
        CheckConstraint("coef_type <> 'DEPT' OR (dept_revenue_amount IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND dept_profit_amount IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND profit_rate IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND dept_allocatable_pool IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND dept_allocatable_pool >= 0)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_coef_dept_source"),
        CheckConstraint("is_default = FALSE OR (default_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(default_reason)) >= 5)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_coef_default"),
    )


class M17PerfResultAudit(Base):
    """m17_perf_result_audit（审核记录）

    只增表：REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_result_audit"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    result_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    audit_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=1)
    audit_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    audit_opinion: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(1000), nullable=False)
    reject_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500))
    verified_depts: Mapped[List[int]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                       default=list)
    audited_by: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    audited_by_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    audited_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_audit_result_seq", "result_id", "audit_seq",
              unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_audit_batch", "calc_batch_no",
              text("audited_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_audit_by", "audited_by", text("audited_at DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("audit_seq >= 1", name="ck_m17_perf_audit_seq"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_result IN ('PASS','REJECT')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_audit_result"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(audit_opinion)) >= 10",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_audit_opinion"),
        CheckConstraint("audit_result <> 'REJECT' OR (reject_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(reject_reason)) >= 10)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_audit_reject"),
    )


class M17PerfPayout(Base):
    """m17_perf_payout（发放台账）

    final_total 由 CHECK 强制等于 original_total + adjust_total。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_payout"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    payout_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    headcount: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    original_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                     default=Decimal("0.00"))
    adjust_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                   default=Decimal("0.00"))
    final_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                                                  default=Decimal("0.00"))

    payout_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False,
                                               default="HR_MERGE")
    hr_batch_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    payout_note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(1000), nullable=False)

    precheck_publish_ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                       default=False)
    precheck_appeal_ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                      default=False)
    precheck_approval_ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False,
                                                        default=False)

    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False, default="DRAFT")
    failure_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500))
    paid_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)

    operator_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    operator_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=False)

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_payout_no", "payout_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_payout_period", "period", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("status = 'PAID' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_payout_period", "period", "status",
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("payout_no ~ '^PY[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{3}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint("payout_method IN ('HR_MERGE','BANK_TRANSFER',"
                        "'DEPT_SECONDARY')", name="ck_m17_perf_payout_method"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('DRAFT','CONFIRMED','SUBMITTED','PAID','FAILED')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_status"),
        CheckConstraint("final_total = original_total + adjust_total",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_close"),
        CheckConstraint("payout_method = 'DEPT_SECONDARY' OR hr_batch_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_hr_batch"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(payout_note)) >= 10",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_note"),
        CheckConstraint("status <> 'PAID' OR (precheck_publish_ok = TRUE "
                        "AND precheck_appeal_ok = TRUE "
                        "AND precheck_approval_ok = TRUE AND paid_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_payout_paid"),
    )


class M17PerfAdjust(Base):
    """m17_perf_adjust（调整与申诉）

    只增表：REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE。
    record_type 区分 APPEAL / ADJUST / REVERSAL。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_perf_adjust"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    record_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    record_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)

    result_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    calc_batch_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    staff_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    staff_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    staff_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    dept_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    # 申诉
    appeal_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    appeal_content: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(2000))
    appeal_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16))
    attachment_urls: Mapped[List[str]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False,
                                                        default=list)
    submitted_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)

    # 处理
    verify_result: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    verify_note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    resolution_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    reply_to_staff: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    resolved_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP)
    resolved_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    resolved_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))

    # 调整
    original_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    adjust_amount: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    final_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(18, 2))
    adjust_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1000))
    adjust_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24))
    appeal_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    reversal_of: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    applied_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    applied_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64))
    applied_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m17_perf_adjust_no", "record_type", "record_no", unique=True),
        Index("uk_m17_perf_appeal_open", "staff_id", "period", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("record_type = 'APPEAL' "
                                    "AND appeal_status IN ('SUBMITTED','PROCESSING')")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_adjust_result", "result_id", "record_type"),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_adjust_period", "period", "record_type",
              text("applied_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_adjust_staff", "staff_id", text("period DESC")),
        Index("idx_m17_perf_appeal_pending", "period", "appeal_status",
              postgresql_where=text("appeal_status IN ('SUBMITTED','PROCESSING')")),
        CheckConstraint("record_type IN ('APPEAL','ADJUST','REVERSAL')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_type"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type <> 'APPEAL' OR (appeal_type IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND appeal_content IS NOT NULL AND appeal_status IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND char_length(btrim(appeal_content)) >= 20 "
                        "AND submitted_at IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_appeal_fields"),
        CheckConstraint("appeal_type IS NULL OR appeal_type IN ('DATA_ERROR',"
                        "'COEF_DISPUTE','FORMULA_DISPUTE','OTHER')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_appeal_type"),
        CheckConstraint("appeal_status IS NULL OR appeal_status IN "
                        "('SUBMITTED','PROCESSING','RESOLVED','REJECTED','WITHDRAWN')",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_appeal_status"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type = 'APPEAL' OR (adjust_amount IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND adjust_reason IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND original_amount_snap IS NOT NULL "
                        "AND final_amount_snap IS NOT NULL)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_fields"),
        # 调整闭合：最终应付快照 = 原绩效快照 + 调整额（下限 0）
        CheckConstraint("record_type = 'APPEAL' OR final_amount_snap = "
                        "GREATEST(original_amount_snap + adjust_amount, 0.00)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_closed"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type = 'APPEAL' OR "
                        "adjust_amount >= -(original_amount_snap)",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_nonneg"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type = 'APPEAL' OR "
                        "(abs(adjust_amount) <= 500.00 AND adjust_amount >= 0) "
                        "OR approval_no IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_approval"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type <> 'REVERSAL' OR reversal_of IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m17_perf_adjust_reversal"),
    )


# 模型与表对照
MODEL_TABLE_MAP = {
    "M17PerfScheme": "m17_perf_scheme",
    "M17PerfIndicator": "m17_perf_indicator",
    "M17PerfCalcBatch": "m17_perf_calc_batch",
    "M17PerfWorkload": "m17_perf_workload",
    "M17PerfResult": "m17_perf_result",
    "M17PerfResultItem": "m17_perf_result_item",
    "M17PerfCoefficient": "m17_perf_coefficient",
    "M17PerfResultAudit": "m17_perf_result_audit",
    "M17PerfPayout": "m17_perf_payout",
    "M17PerfAdjust": "m17_perf_adjust",
}
```

**模型与表对照**

| 模型类 | 表名 | 含义 | 关键约束 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `M17PerfScheme` | `m17_perf_scheme` | 绩效方案主档（版本化） | `uk(scheme_no,version)`、`uk(scheme_no)` WHERE ACTIVE |
| `M17PerfIndicator` | `m17_perf_indicator` | 方案指标与权重 | `uk(scheme_id,indicator_code)`、`uk(scheme_id,sort_no)` |
| `M17PerfCalcBatch` | `m17_perf_calc_batch` | 计算批次 | `uk(calc_batch_no)`、`uk(period,scheme_no)` WHERE DRAFT |
| `M17PerfWorkload` | `m17_perf_workload` | 工作量采集明细 | `uk(calc_batch_no,staff_id,indicator_code)` |
| `M17PerfResult` | `m17_perf_result` | 绩效结果主档 | `uk(calc_batch_no,staff_id)`、`ck_..._amount`（公式） |
| `M17PerfResultItem` | `m17_perf_result_item` | 指标得分明细 | `uk(result_id,indicator_code)`、`ck_..._score_linear` |
| `M17PerfCoefficient` | `m17_perf_coefficient` | 系数快照 | `uk(calc_batch_no,dept_id,coef_type)` |
| `M17PerfResultAudit` | `m17_perf_result_audit` | 审核记录（只增） | `uk(result_id,audit_seq)` |
| `M17PerfPayout` | `m17_perf_payout` | 发放台账 | `uk(payout_no)`、`ck_..._close`（金额闭合） |
| `M17PerfAdjust` | `m17_perf_adjust` | 调整与申诉（只增） | `uk(staff_id,period)` WHERE 未结案申诉、`ck_..._closed` |

### 4.13 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
# app/schemas/m17/performance.py
"""M17-07 绩效核算 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

SchemeStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "ACTIVE", "INACTIVE", "EXPIRED"]
ApplyScope = Literal["ALL", "DEPT", "STAFF_TYPE", "TITLE"]
IndicatorType = Literal["WORKLOAD", "QUALITY", "ECONOMIC", "SERVICE", "MANUAL"]
CalcMethod = Literal["LINEAR", "TIERED", "CAPPED", "DEDUCTION", "THRESHOLD"]
DataSource = Literal[
    "M07_WORKLOAD", "M06_SCHEDULE", "M09_SURGERY", "M16_DAILY",
    "M16_OPTICAL", "M05_CHARGE", "M14_ISSUE", "M15_CONSUME",
    "M11_QUALITY", "M03_SATISFACTION", "MANUAL",
]
SettleStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "CALCULATED", "AUDITED", "PUBLISHED",
                       "PAID", "CANCELLED"]
CollectionStatus = Literal["COMPLETE", "PARTIAL", "MISSING"]
CoefType = Literal["DEPT", "QUALITY"]
PayoutMethod = Literal["HR_MERGE", "BANK_TRANSFER", "DEPT_SECONDARY"]
PayoutStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "CONFIRMED", "SUBMITTED", "PAID", "FAILED"]
RecordType = Literal["APPEAL", "ADJUST", "REVERSAL"]
AppealStatus = Literal["SUBMITTED", "PROCESSING", "RESOLVED", "REJECTED",
                       "WITHDRAWN"]
ResolutionType = Literal["NO_CHANGE", "ADJUST_UP", "ADJUST_DOWN", "RECALC",
                         "DATA_CORRECT"]


# ==================== 方案请求体 ====================

class CoefficientSegment(BaseModel):
    """映射区间元素（科室系数或质量系数共用）。"""

    rate_min: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="区间下限（含），None 表示负无穷")
    rate_max: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="区间上限（不含），None 表示正无穷")
    score_min: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="质量得分下限（含）")
    score_max: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="质量得分上限（不含）")
    coef_from: Decimal = Field(..., description="区间起点系数值")
    coef_to: Decimal = Field(..., description="区间终点系数值")
    fixed: bool = Field(False, description="是否固定值（不做线性插值）")


class SchemeCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新建方案或生成新版本。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True)

    scheme_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                     description="方案号；新建时留空由后端生成")
    base_version: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1,
                                        description="基础版本号；修改已有方案时必填")
    scheme_name: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=64, description="方案名称")
    apply_scope: ApplyScope = Field("DEPT", description="适用范围")
    apply_depts: List[int] = Field(default_factory=list, description="适用科室 ID")
    apply_staff_types: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="适用岗位类型")
    apply_titles: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="适用职称")
    effective_from: date = Field(..., description="生效起始日")
    effective_to: date = Field(..., description="生效截止日")
    pool_ratio: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0.150000"), gt=0, le=Decimal("0.500000"),
                                description="科室结余→可分配池比例")
    retained_ratio_cap: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0.200000"), ge=0, le=1,
                                        description="科室留存比例上限")
    allow_negative_incentive: bool = Field(False, description="是否允许负激励")
    show_rank: bool = Field(False, description="是否向职工展示排名")
    dept_coefficient_mapping: List[CoefficientSegment] = Field(
        ..., min_length=1, description="科室系数映射")
    dept_coef_min: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0.900000"), gt=0, le=Decimal("2.000000"),
                                   description="科室系数硬下限")
    dept_coef_max: Decimal = Field(Decimal("1.100000"), gt=0, le=Decimal("2.000000"),
                                   description="科室系数硬上限")
    quality_coefficient_mapping: List[CoefficientSegment] = Field(
        ..., min_length=1, description="质量系数映射")
    quality_component_weights: Dict[str, Decimal] = Field(
        ..., description="质量分项权重 {efficacy, complication, refund, error}")
    quality_floor_coefficient: Optional[Decimal] = Field(
        None, ge=Decimal("0.850000"), le=Decimal("1.000000"),
        description="质量系数保底值，None 表示不保底")
    change_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1000,
                                         description="变更说明（v2 起必填 ≥10 字）")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1000, description="备注")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_scope(self) -> "SchemeCreateIn":
        """适用范围与清单必须配套。"""
        if self.apply_scope == "DEPT" and not self.apply_depts:
            raise ValueError("范围=DEPT 时必须选择适用科室")
        if self.apply_scope == "STAFF_TYPE" and not self.apply_staff_types:
            raise ValueError("范围=STAFF_TYPE 时必须选择岗位类型")
        if self.apply_scope == "TITLE" and not self.apply_titles:
            raise ValueError("范围=TITLE 时必须选择职称")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_weights(self) -> "SchemeCreateIn":
        """质量分项四项权重合计必须 = 1.000000。"""
        keys = ("efficacy", "complication", "refund", "error")
        missing = [k for k in keys if k not in self.quality_component_weights]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError("缺少质量分项权重：%s" % missing)
        total = sum(self.quality_component_weights[k] for k in keys)
        if abs(total - Decimal("1.000000")) > Decimal("0.000001"):
            raise ValueError("质量分项权重合计必须为 1.000000，当前为 %s" % total)
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_bounds(self) -> "SchemeCreateIn":
        """科室系数硬边界自洽。"""
        if self.dept_coef_max < self.dept_coef_min:
            raise ValueError("科室系数上限不得低于下限")
        return self

    @field_validator("effective_to")
    @classmethod
    def _check_period(cls, v: date, info: Any) -> date:
        """生效期起止顺序与不跨年度。"""
        start = info.data.get("effective_from")
        if start and v < start:
            raise ValueError("生效截止日不得早于起始日")
        if start and v > start.replace(year=start.year + 1):
            raise ValueError("生效期不得跨年度")
        return v


class SchemeToggleIn(BaseModel):
    """启用或停用方案。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="方案版本号")
    action: Literal["ACTIVATE", "DEACTIVATE"] = Field(..., description="动作")
    effective_from: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="ACTIVATE 时的新生效起始日")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500, description="备注")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


# ==================== 指标请求体 ====================

class IndicatorCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新增绩效指标。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True)

    version: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="方案版本号")
    indicator_code: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{1,31}$",
                                description="指标编码")
    indicator_name: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=64, description="指标名称")
    indicator_type: IndicatorType = Field(..., description="指标类型")
    data_source: DataSource = Field(..., description="数据来源")
    source_field: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                        description="上游字段名（MANUAL 时可为空）")
    unit: str = Field(..., max_length=16, description="计量单位")
    unit_value: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0.0000"), ge=0, description="单位分值（元/单位）")
    weight: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0.000000"), ge=0, le=1, description="权重")
    calc_method: CalcMethod = Field("LINEAR", description="计算方式")
    calc_params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="计算参数")
    sort_no: int = Field(1, ge=1, le=99, description="排序号")
    is_enabled: bool = Field(True, description="是否启用")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500, description="备注")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_params(self) -> "IndicatorCreateIn":
        """计算方式与参数必须配套。"""
        if self.calc_method == "LINEAR":
            if self.calc_params is not None:
                raise ValueError("LINEAR 方式不需要 calc_params")
        elif self.calc_params is None:
            raise ValueError("%s 方式必须提供 calc_params" % self.calc_method)
        return self


class IndicatorUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """修改绩效指标。"""

    unit_value: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, description="单位分值")
    weight: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, le=1, description="权重")
    calc_method: Optional[CalcMethod] = Field(None, description="计算方式")
    calc_params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="计算参数")
    is_enabled: Optional[bool] = Field(None, description="是否启用")
    sort_no: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=99, description="排序号")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500, description="备注")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


# ==================== 批次与计算请求体 ====================

class BatchCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """创建结算批次。"""

    period: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$", description="会计期间 YYYY-MM")
    scheme_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="方案号")
    scheme_version: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="方案版本号")
    balance_basis: Literal["CURRENT_MONTH", "LAST_3_MONTH"] = Field(
        "CURRENT_MONTH", description="结余口径")
    quality_basis: Literal["CURRENT_MONTH", "LAST_3_MONTH"] = Field(
        "CURRENT_MONTH", description="质量口径")
    retained_handling: Literal["KEEP_DEPT", "BY_HEADCOUNT"] = Field(
        "KEEP_DEPT", description="科室留存处理方式")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


class CalculateIn(BaseModel):
    """执行绩效计算。"""

    confirm_warnings: bool = Field(False, description="是否确认接受 WARN 级检查项")
    preview_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅预览不落库")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


class WorkloadCollectIn(BaseModel):
    """触发工作量采集。"""

    period: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$", description="会计期间")
    calc_batch_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="计算批次号")
    sources: List[DataSource] = Field(..., min_length=1,
                                      description="采集来源（M06_SCHEDULE 走 P-05 推送）")
    overwrite_existing: bool = Field(False, description="是否覆盖已有数据")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


class SupplementValue(BaseModel):
    """单个指标的补录值。"""

    indicator_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="指标编码")
    actual_value: Decimal = Field(..., ge=0, description="补录实际值")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500, description="说明")


class SupplementIn(BaseModel):
    """人工补录工作量。"""

    handle_type: Literal["ZERO", "MANUAL_VALUE", "SKIP"] = Field(
        ..., description="处理方式：补零/手工录入/跳过")
    missing_reason: Literal["NEW_HIRE", "ON_LEAVE", "TRANSFER",
                            "UPSTREAM_ANOMALY", "OTHER"] = Field(
        ..., description="缺失原因")
    values: List[SupplementValue] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                          description="手工录入值（MANUAL_VALUE 时必填）")
    manual_basis: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=1000,
                              description="补录依据（≥10 字）")
    attachment_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="佐证附件")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_values(self) -> "SupplementIn":
        """手工录入必须提供值。"""
        if self.handle_type == "MANUAL_VALUE" and not self.values:
            raise ValueError("MANUAL_VALUE 方式必须提供至少一条 values")
        return self


# ==================== 审核与公示请求体 ====================

class ResultAuditIn(BaseModel):
    """审核绩效结果。"""

    audit_result: Literal["PASS", "REJECT"] = Field(..., description="审核结论")
    audit_opinion: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=1000,
                               description="审核意见（≥10 字）")
    verified_dept_ids: List[int] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                         description="本次核对过的科室 ID")
    reject_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500,
                                         description="退回原因（REJECT 时必填 ≥10 字）")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_reject(self) -> "ResultAuditIn":
        """退回必须给原因。"""
        if self.audit_result == "REJECT":
            if not self.reject_reason or len(self.reject_reason.strip()) < 10:
                raise ValueError("退回原因不得少于 10 个字（1707018）")
        return self


class PublishIn(BaseModel):
    """公示绩效结果。"""

    publish_start_date: date = Field(..., description="公示起始日")
    publish_end_date: date = Field(..., description="公示截止日")
    publish_channels: List[Literal["SELF_SERVICE", "MESSAGE"]] = Field(
        ..., min_length=1, description="公示渠道")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_period(self) -> "PublishIn":
        """公示期至少 5 个自然日（工作日由服务层按节假日历校验）。"""
        if self.publish_end_date < self.publish_start_date:
            raise ValueError("公示截止日不得早于起始日")
        if (self.publish_end_date - self.publish_start_date).days < 4:
            raise ValueError("公示期不足 5 个日历日（1707019）")
        return self


# ==================== 发放请求体 ====================

class PayoutCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """创建发放批次。"""

    period: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$", description="会计期间")
    calc_batch_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="计算批次号")
    payout_method: PayoutMethod = Field("HR_MERGE", description="发放方式")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


class PayoutConfirmIn(BaseModel):
    """确认发放。"""

    hr_batch_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                       description="人事系统批次号（HR_MERGE/BANK_TRANSFER 必填）")
    payout_note: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=1000,
                             description="发放说明（≥10 字）")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")


# ==================== 申诉请求体 ====================

class AppealCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """提交申诉（职工）。"""

    period: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$", description="会计期间")
    result_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="绩效结果 ID")
    appeal_type: Literal["DATA_ERROR", "COEF_DISPUTE", "FORMULA_DISPUTE",
                         "OTHER"] = Field(..., description="申诉类型")
    appeal_content: str = Field(..., min_length=20, max_length=2000,
                                description="申诉内容（≥20 字）")
    attachment_urls: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=5,
                                       description="佐证附件 URL")


class AppealProcessIn(BaseModel):
    """处理申诉并判定调整。"""

    verify_result: bool = Field(..., description="核查结论：True 属实 / False 不属实")
    verify_note: str = Field(..., min_length=20, max_length=1000,
                             description="核查说明（≥20 字）")
    resolution_type: ResolutionType = Field(..., description="处理结论")
    adjust_method: Optional[Literal["DIRECT_ADJUST", "RECALC"]] = Field(
        None, description="调整方式（ADJUST_UP/ADJUST_DOWN 时必填）")
    adjust_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="调整金额（正调增/负调减）")
    adjust_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1000,
                                         description="调整原因（≥10 字）")
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                       description="M20 审批单号（>500.00 或调减必填）")
    reply_to_staff: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=1000,
                                description="答复职工内容（≥10 字）")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="操作人 ID")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_adjust(self) -> "AppealProcessIn":
        """调整类结论必须带金额与原因。"""
        if self.resolution_type in ("ADJUST_UP", "ADJUST_DOWN"):
            if self.adjust_amount is None or self.adjust_method is None:
                raise ValueError("调整类结论必须提供 adjust_amount 与 adjust_method")
            if not self.adjust_reason or len(self.adjust_reason.strip()) < 10:
                raise ValueError("调整原因不得少于 10 个字（1707024）")
            if self.resolution_type == "ADJUST_UP" and self.adjust_amount <= 0:
                raise ValueError("调增金额必须为正数")
            if self.resolution_type == "ADJUST_DOWN" and self.adjust_amount >= 0:
                raise ValueError("调减金额必须为负数")
        if self.verify_result is False and self.resolution_type != "NO_CHANGE":
            raise ValueError("核查不属实时处理结论只能为 NO_CHANGE")
        return self


# ==================== P-05 推送请求体 ====================

class PerfBaseItem(BaseModel):
    """P-05 推送的单条数据。"""

    staff_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="职工 ID")
    staff_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="工号")
    staff_name: str = Field(..., max_length=64, description="职工姓名")
    dept_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="科室 ID")
    indicator_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="指标编码")
    actual_value: Decimal = Field(..., ge=0, description="指标实际值（非负）")
    unit: str = Field(..., max_length=16, description="计量单位")
    detail: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="明细拆分")


class PerfBasePushIn(BaseModel):
    """P-05 绩效基础数据推送（M06-07 调用）。"""

    period: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}$", description="会计期间")
    dept_id: int = Field(..., ge=1, description="推送科室 ID")
    data_source: DataSource = Field("M06_SCHEDULE", description="数据来源")
    push_batch_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="推送批次号（幂等）")
    pushed_at: datetime = Field(..., description="推送时刻")
    items: List[PerfBaseItem] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=500,
                                      description="推送明细，单次 ≤500 条")

    @field_validator("items")
    @classmethod
    def _no_duplicate(cls, v: List[PerfBaseItem]) -> List[PerfBaseItem]:
        """同一推送内不允许同一人同一指标重复。"""
        keys = [(i.staff_id, i.indicator_code) for i in v]
        if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
            raise ValueError("同一推送内不可出现重复的 (staff_id, indicator_code)")
        return v


# ==================== 响应体 ====================

class SchemeBrief(BaseModel):
    """方案列表行。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    scheme_no: str = Field(..., description="方案号")
    scheme_name: str = Field(..., description="方案名称")
    current_version: int = Field(..., description="当前版本号")
    status: SchemeStatus = Field(..., description="方案状态")
    apply_scope: ApplyScope = Field(..., description="适用范围")
    apply_dept_count: int = Field(..., description="适用科室数")
    effective_from: date = Field(..., description="生效起始日")
    effective_to: date = Field(..., description="生效截止日")
    indicator_count: int = Field(..., description="指标总数")
    enabled_indicator_count: int = Field(..., description="启用指标数")
    pool_ratio: Decimal = Field(..., description="分配比例")
    created_at: datetime = Field(..., description="创建时间")


class SchemeDetail(SchemeBrief):
    """方案详情（含映射与权重）。"""

    previous_version: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="上一版本号")
    apply_depts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="适用科室清单")
    retained_ratio_cap: Decimal = Field(..., description="留存比例上限")
    allow_negative_incentive: bool = Field(..., description="是否允许负激励")
    dept_coefficient_mapping: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="科室系数映射")
    dept_coef_bounds: Dict[str, Decimal] = Field(..., description="科室系数硬边界")
    quality_coefficient_mapping: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="质量系数映射")
    quality_component_weights: Dict[str, Decimal] = Field(..., description="质量分项权重")
    quality_floor_coefficient: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="质量系数保底值")
    change_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="变更说明")


class IndicatorOut(BaseModel):
    """指标出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int = Field(..., description="指标 ID")
    indicator_code: str = Field(..., description="指标编码")
    indicator_name: str = Field(..., description="指标名称")
    indicator_type: IndicatorType = Field(..., description="指标类型")
    data_source: DataSource = Field(..., description="数据来源")
    source_field: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="上游字段名")
    unit: str = Field(..., description="计量单位")
    unit_value: Decimal = Field(..., description="单位分值")
    weight: Decimal = Field(..., description="权重")
    calc_method: CalcMethod = Field(..., description="计算方式")
    calc_params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(None, description="计算参数")
    sort_no: int = Field(..., description="排序号")
    is_enabled: bool = Field(..., description="是否启用")


class BatchBrief(BaseModel):
    """批次列表行。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    calc_batch_no: str = Field(..., description="批次号")
    settle_no: str = Field(..., description="结算单号")
    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    scheme_no: str = Field(..., description="方案号")
    scheme_version: str = Field(..., description="方案版本")
    status: SettleStatus = Field(..., description="批次状态")
    headcount: int = Field(..., description="参与人数")
    total_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="Σ个人绩效")
    retained_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="科室留存合计")
    avg_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="人均绩效")
    is_recalc: bool = Field(..., description="是否重算批次")
    superseded_batch_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="被取代批次号")
    started_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="开始时刻")
    finished_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="完成时刻")
    duration_ms: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="耗时（毫秒）")


class WorkloadOut(BaseModel):
    """工作量采集明细出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int = Field(..., description="明细 ID")
    staff_id: int = Field(..., description="职工 ID")
    staff_no: str = Field(..., description="工号")
    staff_name: str = Field(..., description="职工姓名（脱敏）")
    dept_id: int = Field(..., description="科室 ID")
    dept_name: str = Field(..., description="科室名称")
    indicator_code: str = Field(..., description="指标编码")
    indicator_name: str = Field(..., description="指标名称")
    actual_value: Decimal = Field(..., description="实际值")
    unit: str = Field(..., description="计量单位")
    data_source: Optional[DataSource] = Field(None, description="数据来源")
    collection_status: CollectionStatus = Field(..., description="采集状态")
    collected_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="采集时刻")
    is_manual: bool = Field(..., description="是否人工补录")
    manual_basis: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="补录依据")


class ResultBrief(BaseModel):
    """绩效结果列表行。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int = Field(..., description="结果 ID")
    staff_id: int = Field(..., description="职工 ID")
    staff_no: str = Field(..., description="工号")
    staff_name: str = Field(..., description="职工姓名（脱敏）")
    dept_id: int = Field(..., description="科室 ID")
    dept_name: str = Field(..., description="科室名称")
    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    base_subtotal: Decimal = Field(..., description="基础小计")
    dept_coefficient: Decimal = Field(..., description="科室系数")
    quality_coefficient: Decimal = Field(..., description="质量调整系数")
    original_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="原绩效")
    adjust_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="调整额")
    final_amount: Decimal = Field(..., description="最终应付")
    dept_share_ratio: Decimal = Field(..., description="占科室比")
    result_status: SettleStatus = Field(..., description="结果状态")
    is_audited: bool = Field(..., description="是否已审核")
    has_manual_workload: bool = Field(..., description="是否含人工补录")
    has_quality_default: bool = Field(..., description="是否使用默认质量系数")


class ResultItemOut(BaseModel):
    """指标得分明细出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    indicator_code: str = Field(..., description="指标编码")
    indicator_name: str = Field(..., description="指标名称")
    actual_value: Decimal = Field(..., description="实际值")
    unit: str = Field(..., description="计量单位")
    unit_value: Decimal = Field(..., description="单位分值")
    weight: Decimal = Field(..., description="权重")
    calc_method: CalcMethod = Field(..., description="计算方式")
    score: Decimal = Field(..., description="指标得分")
    formula_text: str = Field(..., description="计算式文字")
    data_source: Optional[DataSource] = Field(None, description="数据来源")
    collected_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="采集时刻")


class ResultDetail(ResultBrief):
    """绩效结果详情（含明细与追溯链）。"""

    scheme_no: str = Field(..., description="方案号")
    scheme_version: str = Field(..., description="方案版本")
    calc_batch_no: str = Field(..., description="批次号")
    items: List[ResultItemOut] = Field(..., description="指标得分明细")
    coefficient_trace: Dict[str, Any] = Field(..., description="系数追溯链")
    adjusts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="调整记录")
    computed_verified: bool = Field(..., description="是否已按明细重算校验一致")


class P10ResultOut(BaseModel):
    """P-10 绩效结果查询出参。"""

    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    calc_batch_no: str = Field(..., description="批次号")
    scheme_no: str = Field(..., description="方案号")
    scheme_version: str = Field(..., description="方案版本")
    batch_status: SettleStatus = Field(..., description="批次状态")
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(..., description="结果列表")
    total: int = Field(..., description="总条数")
    page: int = Field(..., description="当前页")
    page_size: int = Field(..., description="每页条数")
    pages: int = Field(..., description="总页数")
    summary: Dict[str, Any] = Field(..., description="汇总（人数/金额合计/系数）")
    availability: Literal["AVAILABLE", "NOT_FINALIZED"] = Field(
        ..., description="数据可用性")
    data_freshness: Literal["REALTIME", "FINALIZED"] = Field(
        ..., description="数据新鲜度")
```

**Schema 与用途对照**

| Schema | 用途 | 对应接口 |
|---|---|---|
| `SchemeCreateIn` | 新建方案/生成新版本入参 | API-07 |
| `SchemeToggleIn` | 启用/停用入参 | API-08 |
| `IndicatorCreateIn` | 新增指标入参 | API-10 |
| `IndicatorUpdateIn` | 修改指标入参 | API-11 |
| `BatchCreateIn` | 创建结算批次入参 | API-17 |
| `CalculateIn` | 执行计算入参 | API-19 |
| `WorkloadCollectIn` | 触发采集入参 | API-14 |
| `SupplementIn` | 人工补录入参 | API-15 |
| `ResultAuditIn` | 审核入参 | API-24 |
| `PublishIn` | 公示入参 | API-25 |
| `PayoutCreateIn` | 创建发放批次入参 | API-27 |
| `PayoutConfirmIn` | 确认发放入参 | API-28 |
| `AppealCreateIn` | 提交申诉入参 | API-30 |
| `AppealProcessIn` | 处理申诉入参 | API-31 |
| `PerfBasePushIn` | **P-05 推送入参** | API-33 |
| `SchemeBrief` / `SchemeDetail` | 方案列表行 / 详情出参 | API-05 / API-06 |
| `IndicatorOut` | 指标出参 | API-09 |
| `BatchBrief` | 批次列表行出参 | API-16 |
| `WorkloadOut` | 工作量明细出参 | API-13 |
| `ResultBrief` / `ResultDetail` | 结果列表行 / 详情出参 | API-21 / API-22 |
| `P10ResultOut` | **P-10 查询出参** | API-34 |
